PCBoard PPE Collection

PPL Executable Archive — 6,590 unique PPEs for PCBoard 15.x

Combined archive: pcbppes.zip (2,757) + Roy/SAC (4,548) — 6,590 unique

PPEs (PCBoard Programming Language Executables) are compiled scripts that extend PCBoard with custom doors, utilities, games, login screens, prompts, and system tools. Each ZIP contains the .PPE binary and usually a FILE_ID.DIZ, documentation, and ANSI screens.

To install a PPE: unzip to your PPE directory, configure in PCBSETUP or reference from CMD.LST, SCRIPT files, or @-code display files.
